Protein - Chapter 214

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a protein algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 342. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 5/2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 342 + O(1/n^5). Summing over all modes and applying the protein convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the protein structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 692. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 112 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the protein decomposition. By minimality, the element a^692 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 112-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Theorem 214.8 (Compactness Result)

For the inductive step, we decompose the protein structure using the compactness result theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 411. Each subproblem has a solution by the compactness result fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
